In Command Prompt (Win + R), to start a new MySQL client with --local-infile:

"C:\Program Files\MySQL\MySQL Server 8.0\bin\mysql.exe" --local-infile=1 -u root -p 
And then enter the password of MySQL - 12345 

This will keep the local_infile 
how variables like 'local_infile'

mysql> CREATE TABLE consumer_complaints (
    ->     complaint_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    ->     date_received DATE NOT NULL,
    ->     product VARCHAR(100),
    ->     sub_product VARCHAR(100),
    ->     issue VARCHAR(150),
    ->     sub_issue VARCHAR(255),
    ->     consumer_complaint_narrative MEDIUMTEXT,
    ->     company_public_response VARCHAR(255),
    ->     company VARCHAR(150),
    ->     state VARCHAR(50),
    ->     zip_code VARCHAR(10),
    ->     tags VARCHAR(50),
    ->     submitted_via VARCHAR(30),
    ->     date_sent_to_company DATE,
    ->     company_response_to_consumer VARCHAR(100),
    ->     timely_response VARCHAR(10),

    -> );

mysql> show create table consumer_complaints;
+---------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Table               | Create Table                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
+---------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| consumer_complaints | CREATE TABLE `consumer_complaints` (
  `complaint_id` bigint NOT NULL,
  `date_received` date NOT NULL,
  `product` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sub_product` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `issue` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sub_issue` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `consumer_complaint_narrative` mediumtext,
  `company_public_response` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `company` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `state` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `zip_code` varchar(10) DEFAULT NULL,
  `tags` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `submitted_via` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `date_sent_to_company` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `company_response_to_consumer` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `timely_response` varchar(10) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`complaint_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci |
+---------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
